{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CgPPy7XD5XC1TomidWgdE","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-ReportMonthly.md ===\n# FEATURE: Report Monthly\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261025 V1 |\n| Description | Implements `ledger report monthly`, which prints inflow, outflow, and net per calendar month computed from stored transactions. |\n| Depends On | ARCHITECTURE.md, DATABASE.md, FEATURE-CliFoundation.md |\n| Provides | ledger report monthly |\n| Consumes | transactions.list |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`ledger report monthly` summarizes cash movement by month. Every value is computed at run time from the stored transactions; no row, count, or total is embedded.\n\n## Trigger\n\n`./ledger report monthly` with no further arguments.\n\n## Behavior\n\n1. List all transactions through `transactions.list` (no account filter).\n2. Group by the `YYYY-MM` prefix of `date`.\n3. For each month that has at least one transaction, in ascending month order, compute in integer cents:\n   - `transactions`: the number of transactions in the month\n   - `inflow`: sum of positive amounts\n   - `outflow`: sum of absolute values of negative amounts, written as a positive number\n   - `net`: `inflow - outflow`\n4. Write the header `month,transactions,inflow,outflow,net`, one row per month, then `TOTAL,<count>,<inflow>,<outflow>,<net>` summed over all transactions.\n5. Amounts are written with exactly two decimal places, a leading `-` when negative, and no thousands separator. No floating point is used.\n6. Months with no transactions are omitted. With no transactions at all, only the header and a zero `TOTAL` row are written.\n\n## Exit Codes\n\n| Code | Condition |\n|------|-----------|\n| 0 | Report written |\n| 1 | Database unreachable or tables missing |\n| 2 | Wrong argument count after `report monthly` |\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-TRANSACTIONS-003` — read: list transactions - supplies: `account` - returns: `txn_id`, `date`, `account`, `category`, `amount`, `memo`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC report-monthly-rows ===\nIntent: The monthly report computes count, inflow, outflow, and net for a month from stored rows and places the month row in ascending order before TOTAL.\n\nimport csv\nimport io\nfrom decimal import Decimal\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\nacct = \"UTRMA1\"\ncat = \"UTRMC1\"\nearly = \"2098-05\"\nlate = \"2098-06\"\nearly_amounts = [10000, -2550]\nlate_amounts = [4001]\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=acct, name=\"Monthly Probe\")\n    db.categories.create(category=cat, description=\"Monthly Probe\")\n    next_id = 920001\n    for month, amounts in ((early, early_amounts), (late, late_amounts)):\n        for cents in amounts:\n            db.transactions.create(\n                txn_id=next_id, date=month + \"-10\", account=acct,\n                category=cat, amount=cents, memo=\"m\",\n            )\n            next_id += 1\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"report\", \"monthly\")\n    print(out)\n    print(err)\n    assert code == 0\n    rows = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(out)))\n    assert rows[0] == [\"month\", \"transactions\", \"inflow\", \"outflow\", \"net\"]\n    assert rows[-1][0] == \"TOTAL\"\n    body = rows[1:-1]\n    months = [r[0] for r in body]\n    assert months == sorted(months)\n    by_month = {r[0]: r for r in body}\n    for month, amounts in ((early, early_amounts), (late, late_amounts)):\n        row = by_month[month]\n        inflow = sum(a for a in amounts if a > 0)\n        outflow = sum(-a for a in amounts if a < 0)\n        assert int(row[1]) == len(amounts)\n        assert Decimal(row[2]) == Decimal(inflow) / 100\n        assert Decimal(row[3]) == Decimal(outflow) / 100\n        assert Decimal(row[4]) == Decimal(inflow - outflow) / 100\n        for cell in row[2:]:\n            assert len(cell.split(\".\")[1]) == 2\n=== END AC report-monthly-rows ===\n\n=== AC report-monthly-total ===\nIntent: The TOTAL row equals the sum of the month rows for count, inflow, outflow, and net.\n\nimport csv\nimport io\nfrom decimal import Decimal\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\nacct = \"UTRMA2\"\ncat = \"UTRMC2\"\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=acct, name=\"Monthly Total\")\n    db.categories.create(category=cat, description=\"Monthly Total\")\n    db.transactions.create(\n        txn_id=920101, date=\"2098-07-01\", account=acct, category=cat, amount=12345, memo=\"a\"\n    )\n    db.transactions.create(\n        txn_id=920102, date=\"2098-08-01\", account=acct, category=cat, amount=-345, memo=\"b\"\n    )\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"report\", \"monthly\")\n    print(out)\n    print(err)\n    assert code == 0\n    rows = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(out)))\n    body, total = rows[1:-1], rows[-1]\n    assert total[0] == \"TOTAL\"\n    assert int(total[1]) == sum(int(r[1]) for r in body)\n    for col in (2, 3, 4):\n        assert Decimal(total[col]) == sum(Decimal(r[col]) for r in body)\n    assert Decimal(total[4]) == Decimal(total[2]) - BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-ReportMonthly.md ===\n# FEATURE: Report Monthly\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261025 V1 |\n| Description | Implements `ledger report monthly`, which prints inflow, outflow, and net per calendar month computed from stored transactions. |\n| Depends On | ARCHITECTURE.md, DATABASE.md, FEATURE-CliFoundation.md |\n| Provides | ledger report monthly |\n| Consumes | transactions.list |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`ledger report monthly` summarizes cash movement by month. Every value is computed at run time from the stored transactions; no row, count, or total is embedded.\n\n## Trigger\n\n`./ledger report monthly` with no further arguments.\n\n## Behavior\n\n1. List all transactions through `transactions.list` (no account filter).\n2. Group by the `YYYY-MM` prefix of `date`.\n3. For each month that has at least one transaction, in ascending month order, compute in integer cents:\n   - `transactions`: the number of transactions in the month\n   - `inflow`: sum of positive amounts\n   - `outflow`: sum of absolute values of negative amounts, written as a positive number\n   - `net`: `inflow - outflow`\n4. Write the header `month,transactions,inflow,outflow,net`, one row per month, then `TOTAL,<count>,<inflow>,<outflow>,<net>` summed over all transactions.\n5. Amounts are written with exactly two decimal places, a leading `-` when negative, and no thousands separator. No floating point is used.\n6. Months with no transactions are omitted. With no transactions at all, only the header and a zero `TOTAL` row are written.\n\n## Exit Codes\n\n| Code | Condition |\n|------|-----------|\n| 0 | Report written |\n| 1 | Database unreachable or tables missing |\n| 2 | Wrong argument count after `report monthly` |\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-TRANSACTIONS-003` — read: list transactions - supplies: `account` - returns: `txn_id`, `date`, `account`, `category`, `amount`, `memo`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC report-monthly-rows ===\nIntent: The monthly report computes count, inflow, outflow, and net for a month from stored rows and places the month row in ascending order before TOTAL.\n\nimport csv\nimport io\nfrom decimal import Decimal\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\nacct = \"UTRMA1\"\ncat = \"UTRMC1\"\nearly = \"2098-05\"\nlate = \"2098-06\"\nearly_amounts = [10000, -2550]\nlate_amounts = [4001]\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=acct, name=\"Monthly Probe\")\n    db.categories.create(category=cat, description=\"Monthly Probe\")\n    next_id = 920001\n    for month, amounts in ((early, early_amounts), (late, late_amounts)):\n        for cents in amounts:\n            db.transactions.create(\n                txn_id=next_id, date=month + \"-10\", account=acct,\n                category=cat, amount=cents, memo=\"m\",\n            )\n            next_id += 1\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"report\", \"monthly\")\n    print(out)\n    print(err)\n    assert code == 0\n    rows = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(out)))\n    assert rows[0] == [\"month\", \"transactions\", \"inflow\", \"outflow\", \"net\"]\n    assert rows[-1][0] == \"TOTAL\"\n    body = rows[1:-1]\n    months = [r[0] for r in body]\n    assert months == sorted(months)\n    by_month = {r[0]: r for r in body}\n    for month, amounts in ((early, early_amounts), (late, late_amounts)):\n        row = by_month[month]\n        inflow = sum(a for a in amounts if a > 0)\n        outflow = sum(-a for a in amounts if a < 0)\n        assert int(row[1]) == len(amounts)\n        assert Decimal(row[2]) == Decimal(inflow) / 100\n        assert Decimal(row[3]) == Decimal(outflow) / 100\n        assert Decimal(row[4]) == Decimal(inflow - outflow) / 100\n        for cell in row[2:]:\n            assert len(cell.split(\".\")[1]) == 2\n=== END AC report-monthly-rows ===\n\n=== AC report-monthly-total ===\nIntent: The TOTAL row equals the sum of the month rows for count, inflow, outflow, and net.\n\nimport csv\nimport io\nfrom decimal import Decimal\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\nacct = \"UTRMA2\"\ncat = \"UTRMC2\"\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=acct, name=\"Monthly Total\")\n    db.categories.create(category=cat, description=\"Monthly Total\")\n    db.transactions.create(\n        txn_id=920101, date=\"2098-07-01\", account=acct, category=cat, amount=12345, memo=\"a\"\n    )\n    db.transactions.create(\n        txn_id=920102, date=\"2098-08-01\", account=acct, category=cat, amount=-345, memo=\"b\"\n    )\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"report\", \"monthly\")\n    print(out)\n    print(err)\n    assert code == 0\n    rows = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(out)))\n    body, total = rows[1:-1], rows[-1]\n    assert total[0] == \"TOTAL\"\n    assert int(total[1]) == sum(int(r[1]) for r in body)\n    for col in (2, 3, 4):\n        assert Decimal(total[col]) == sum(Decimal(r[col]) for r in body)\n    assert Decimal(total[4]) == Decimal(total[2]) - Decimal(total[3])\n=== END AC report-monthly-total ===\n\n=== AC report-monthly-total-count ===\nIntent: The TOTAL transaction count equals the number of stored transactions read back from the database.\n\nimport csv\nimport io\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"report\", \"monthly\")\n    print(out)\n    print(err)\n    assert code == 0\n    rows = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(out)))\n    assert rows[-1][0] == \"TOTAL\"\n    assert int(rows[-1][1]) == len(db.transactions.list())\n=== END AC report-monthly-total-count ===\n\n=== AC report-monthly-usage ===\nIntent: Extra arguments and an unknown report name exit 2 with nothing on standard output.\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\n\ncode, out, err = cli_runner(\"report\", \"monthly\", \"extra\")\nassert code == 2\nassert out == \"\"\ncode, out, err = cli_runner(\"report\", \"nosuchreport\")\nassert code == 2\nassert out == \"\"\n=== END AC report-monthly-usage ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- Every value is computed from stored rows; no month, count, or total is hardcoded.\n- Amounts are integer cents internally; floating point is never used.\n- Failures write nothing to standard output.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-AccountAdd.md ===\n# FEATURE: Account Add\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261025 V1 |\n| Description | Implements `ledger account add`, which validates and stores a new account and prints its row. |\n| Depends On | ARCHITECTURE.md, DATABASE.md, FEATURE-CliFoundation.md, FEATURE-BalanceRules.md |\n| Provides | ledger account add |\n| Consumes | accounts.create, accounts.get, account_summary |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nAdd a cash account (drawer, safe, petty cash) to the ledger.\n\n## Trigger\n\n`./ledger account add <code> <name>`\n\n## Behavior\n\n1. Require exactly two arguments after `account add`; otherwise exit 2.\n2. Validate the code (1 to 12 characters from `A-Z` and `0-9`) and the name (1 to 40 characters, no comma or newline). Invalid input exits 3.\n3. If `accounts.get` finds the code, exit 3 (duplicate key). Nothing is written.\n4. Store the account with `accounts.create`.\n5. Print the account row from `account_summary`: header `account,name,transactions,balance`, then the code, name, transaction count, and balance. A new account has zero transactions and balance written with two decimals.\n\nAll checks complete before the single write.\n\n## Exit Codes\n\n| Code | Condition |\n|------|-----------|\n| 0 | Account stored |\n| 1 | Database unreachable |\n| 2 | Wrong argument count |\n| 3 | Invalid code or name, or duplicate code |\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-ACCOUNTS-001` — write: create an account - supplies: `account`, `name` - returns: `account`, `name`\n- `FEATURE-ACCOUNTS-002` — read: get an account by code - supplies: `account` - returns: `account`, `name`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC account-add-stores ===\nIntent: Adding an account exits 0, prints the account row with zero transactions, and persists the account.\n\nimport csv\nimport io\nfrom decimal import output.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-AccountDelete.md ===\n# FEATURE: Account Delete\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261025 V1 |\n| Description | Implements `ledger account delete`, which removes an account that has no transactions. |\n| Depends On | ARCHITECTURE.md, DATABASE.md, FEATURE-CliFoundation.md, FEATURE-BalanceRules.md |\n| Provides | ledger account delete |\n| Consumes | accounts.get, accounts.delete, transactions.list |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nRemove an unused account. An account that still has transactions is protected.\n\n## Trigger\n\n`./ledger account delete <code>`\n\n## Behavior\n\n1. Require exactly one argument after `account delete`; otherwise exit 2.\n2. Look up the account with `accounts.get`. An absent code exits 4.\n3. List the account's transactions with `transactions.list`. If any exist, exit 3 and remove nothing.\n4. Delete the account with `accounts.delete`.\n5. Print `deleted` and then the code, one per line.\n\n## Exit Codes\n\n| Code | Condition |\n|------|-----------|\n| 0 | Account deleted |\n| 1 | Database unreachable |\n| 2 | Wrong argument count |\n| 3 | Account has transactions |\n| 4 | Account absent |\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-ACCOUNTS-002` — read: get an account by code - supplies: `account` - returns: `account`, `name`\n- `FEATURE-ACCOUNTS-005` — write: delete an account - supplies: `account` - returns: `account`\n- `FEATURE-TRANSACTIONS-003` — read: list transactions for an account - supplies: `account` - returns: `txn_id`, `date`, `account`, `category`, `amount`, `memo`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC account-delete-unused ===\nIntent: Deleting an account with no transactions exits 0, prints deleted and the code, and removes it.\n\nimport csv\nimport io\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\nacct = \"UTDEL1\"\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=acct, name=\"Delete Probe\")\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"account\", \"delete\", acct)\n    print(out)\n    print(err)\n    assert code == 0\n    rows = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(out)))\n    assert rows == [[\"deleted\"], [acct]]\n    assert db.accounts.get(account=acct) is None\n=== END AC account-delete-unused ===\n\n=== AC account-delete-in-use description=\"Delete Probe\")\n    db.transactions.create(\n        txn_id=txn_id, date=\"2098-01-01\", account=acct, category=cat, amount=900, memo=\"d\"\n    )\n    code, out, err =
